# DevOps and Cloud Engineering: 3+ years, India

Also called: Cloud Engineer, DevOps Engineer, Cloud Support Engineer, Azure DevOps Engineer, System Administrator, Build and Release Engineer, Cloud Administrator, Cloud Operations Engineer, Cloud Infrastructure Engineer, Cloud & DevOps Consultant, Cloud Architect, Azure Engineer, Site Reliability Engineer, Platform Engineer.

Roles that ask for 3 or more years, or are Mid, Senior or Staff level.

- Every role comes from the company's own career page, not from job boards or agencies.
- Every role here was seen open in the last 48 hours and was posted or reopened in the last 14 days. New roles come first, then reopened ones.
- First posted is the date the role first went up. A role that closed and came back says reopened, with both dates.
- At most 5 roles per company in each section; the rest are counted below it.
- Click a role to read it on ASAI: what the post asks for, when it first went up, how often it came back, and who to ask for a referral there. **Apply** goes straight to the company.
- Generated 2026-11-27 06:35 UTC (2026-11-27 12:05 IST). Roles that close are removed at each run.
- Years asked is shown only when the post states it. Pay is not shown.
- Want this list matched to your own resume? Upload it at [asaitech.in](https://www.asaitech.in/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=list&utm_campaign=experienced).
